# Skills · S · part 245

863 entries, sql-review to sqlite-patterns. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **sql-review**: Review SQL, plain or in a dbt project, on any warehouse, and investigate suspicious aggregations, duplicates or business results. Triggers: the number is wrong, duplicates, total that doesn't add up, review this query, join that multiplies rows, unexpected NULLs. Not for: a query that is only slow or expensive, or a metric still to define. ([hachemsoft/ia-toolkit](https://github.com/hachemsoft/ia-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/internal/analytics/sql-review) · ★4)
- **sql-review**: Review SQL joins, aggregations, NULL semantics and query plans without changing production databases. SQL 쿼리 조인 집계 검토. ([himomohi/jev-skill-router](https://github.com/himomohi/jev-skill-router/tree/HEAD/examples/skills/sql-review) · ★4)
- **sql-review**: MySQL SQL代码审查。在SQL提交或部署前，对SQL语句进行静态分析，识别潜在的性能风险、安全问题和反模式。 当用户说"帮我审查SQL"、"review这个SQL"、"这个SQL有没有问题"、"SQL上线前检查"、"SQL代码审查"、"看看这个SQL写得对不对"时触发。 即使用户只是粘贴了一段SQL并问"这样写有问题吗"、"有没有优化空间"，也应该触发此skill。 只要用户提供了SQL语句并希望得到反馈，就应该触发此skill。 ([rizxfrog/insightful](https://github.com/rizxfrog/insightful/tree/HEAD/skills/sql-review) · ★4)
- **sql-review**: Review SQL migrations and queries in ANY repo for safety, RLS correctness, function security, privilege drift, concurrency races, blanket grants, and query quality. DB/ORM-agnostic. Reports ✅/⚠️/❌ per check; optional --gold emits verified findings as portable training pairs. Use when reviewing a .sql migration, an ORM schema (Prisma/Drizzle/Kysely), or a raw query — especially before deploying schema changes to a multi-tenant or user-data app. ([treebird7/treebird-oss-skills](https://github.com/treebird7/treebird-oss-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/skill-sql-review/skills/sql-review) · ★4)
- **sql-review**: Check BigQuery SQL and dbt models for cost best practices with `followrabbit sql`, judge each finding against the actual query, and report only what matters. Use when the user wants BigQuery SQL or dbt models checked for cost antipatterns before commit, or is editing BigQuery SQL or dbt models and mentions cost, scans, partitions, or slots. The checks are deterministic: no LLM call on the Rabbit side and no LLM quota used. ([followrabbit-ai/awesome-rabbit](https://github.com/followrabbit-ai/awesome-rabbit/tree/HEAD/skills/sql-review) · ★3)
- **sql-review**: Reviews or writes SQL for correctness, performance, and the footguns that corrupt data or take a table offline. Use when writing a query, reviewing a migration, or before running an UPDATE or DELETE against production. ([skilletmd/skillet](https://github.com/skilletmd/skillet/tree/HEAD/skills/sql-review) · ★3)
- **sql-review**: Review SQL/dbt models for correctness and full-scan footguns. ([Tarekkharsa/agentstack](https://github.com/Tarekkharsa/agentstack/tree/HEAD/examples/sandbox/projects/data-pipeline/skills/sql-review) · ★3)

- **sql-review**: Review a SQL query for correctness first and performance second — join fan-out inflating sums, NULL traps in filters and NOT IN, timezone and date-boundary errors, integer division, DISTINCT hiding a bad join, window-frame defaults, implicit casts, non-sargable predicates and missing partition filters — with each finding tied to a line, the wrong number it produces, and corrected SQL for the named dialect. Use when the user pastes a query and asks "is this right", "review my SQL", "why is this number too high", "check this before it goes in the dashboard", or "make this query faster". ([sananthanarayan/skilldrop](https://github.com/sananthanarayan/skilldrop/tree/HEAD/packs/data-analytics/skills/sql-review) · ★2)
- **sql-review**: Review a query for correctness, grain, and safety, without running it against a database you were not given. - **sql-review**: Pre-commit review of uncommitted SQL against a catalog of antipatterns that cause real production incidents, reporting BLOCKER / WARN / INFO with file-and-line citations and a fix per finding. Never edits SQL. Use this skill whenever the user says "/sql-review", "review my SQL", "review the SQL diff", "lint the SQL", "check my SQL changes", "SQL pre-commit check", "audit my stored proc", or "any SQL antipatterns in this diff" - even if they do not name the skill. Distinct from code-review (general review) - this carries the SQL-specific incident patterns. - **sql-safety**: Parameterized queries only. PostgreSQL/SQLite type discipline. Idempotent migrations via ON CONFLICT. Use when writing database code, designing migrations, or reviewing SQL. Triggers: SQL injection, parameterized query, migration, ON CONFLICT, schema, Postgres, SQLite, DROP TABLE, idempotent. ([ruddyribera-ops/opencode-power-setup](https://github.com/ruddyribera-ops/opencode-power-setup/tree/HEAD/skills/sql-safety) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **sql-safety-review**: 评估 SQL 查询的安全性、规范性和性能风险。覆盖 OLAP（Spark/Hive、Impala、ClickHouse）和 OLTP（MySQL）两大场景。审查维度：命名规范、编码格式、查询优化、索引策略、DDL/DML 规范、引擎特性、锁与事务。当用户贴出 SQL 问"能不能跑"、"会不会查爆"、"帮我 review"、"格式对不对"、"命名规范吗"、"这个 EXPLAIN 怎么看"、"为什么这么慢"、"能不能在生产跑"、"帮我看看这条 SQL"、"这个查询有没有问题"、"Code Review 一下"、"建表语句有问题吗"、"ETL 上线前审查"、"慢查询优化"、"索引怎么建"、"会不会死锁"、"大批量删除怎么做"时触发。即使用户只是贴了一条 SQL 没说别的，也应触发。支持 SELECT/DDL/DML/EXPLAIN 输出分析。 ([winhok/testkit](https://github.com/winhok/testkit/tree/HEAD/skills/sql-safety-review) · ★101)

- **sql-schema-audit**: Index coverage and N+1 review aids for SQLite/D1 schemas with a sqlc catalog. ([mizchi/skills](https://github.com/mizchi/skills/tree/HEAD/sql-schema-audit) · ★423 · 2 copies)
- **sql-schema-design**: Reviews foundational SQL Server schema design before migration-safety, performance, or injection-defense checks even apply. Flags inconsistent table/column naming conventions, an undocumented surrogate-vs-natural key strategy, missing foreign key constraints enforced only in application code, missing NOT NULL/CHECK constraints on columns with a bounded business domain, stored procedures/views not checked into source control, and unbounded NVARCHAR(MAX) columns on well-known bounded domains. Outputs findings with sql-schema-design standard IDs. ([AgenticPawan/FullStack-Pilot](https://github.com/AgenticPawan/FullStack-Pilot/tree/HEAD/plugins/pilot-sql/skills/sql-schema-design) · ★2)
- **sql-schema-design**: Guides PostgreSQL, MySQL, and SQLite schema design in DDL and migrations: primary and foreign keys, constraints, NOT NULL, data types, normalization, naming, and safe schema changes. Query cost and index selection use sql-performance. ([kohdice/dotfiles](https://github.com/kohdice/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/config/agents/skills/sql-schema-design))
- **sql-schema-design**: Design data schemas for SQL Server, prioritizing integrity, normalization, and performance. Use when the task involves tables, columns, PKs, FKs, constraints, relationships, structural indexes, or schema migrations. ([MiracleDevs/Paradigm.Enterprise](https://github.com/MiracleDevs/Paradigm.Enterprise/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/database-skills/sql-schema-design))
- **sql-schema-design**: Use when designing relational database schemas: normalization, primary/foreign keys, indexes, constraints, migrations, and modeling relationships (1:1, 1:N, M:N) for a given domain. ([PouriaNajarian/agent-template](https://github.com/PouriaNajarian/agent-template/tree/HEAD/data-engineer-agent/skills/sql-schema-design) · 2 copies)
- **sql-schema-design-and-normalization**: Guides portable relational schema design — normalize to remove the insertion, update, and deletion anomalies (the 1NF→BCNF ladder), choose natural vs surrogate keys deliberately, model many-to-many with a junction table, and denormalize only with a stated reason. Bans the structural antipatterns that no constraint can later rescue — comma-separated value lists ("Jaywalking", a First Normal Form violation), Entity-Attribute-Value (EAV) key-value rows instead of columns, adjacency-only "Naive Trees" that cannot query a subtree, and polymorphic/promiscuous foreign keys that carry no referential… ([ctoth/sql-skills-plugin](https://github.com/ctoth/sql-skills-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/sql/skills/sql-schema-design-and-normalization) · ★2)
- **sql-schema-design-best-practices**: Stack-agnostic standard for designing and evolving relational database schemas. - **sql-window-functions**: Guides window functions — the highest-leverage modern-SQL technique and the frame-clause traps LLMs reliably botch. A window function computes across related rows without collapsing them (PARTITION BY is not GROUP BY). The centerpiece — the default frame with ORDER BY is RANGE BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW, which makes LAST_VALUE return the current row, not the partition's last, and makes running totals jump on tied sort keys; fix with an explicit ROWS frame (or MAX() OVER). Covers ROW_NUMBER/RANK/DENSE_RANK/NTILE tie behavior, LAG/LEAD offsets, FIRST_VALUE/LAST_VALUE/NTH_VALUE,… ([ctoth/sql-skills-plugin](https://github.com/ctoth/sql-skills-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/sql/skills/sql-window-functions) · ★2)

Use for rankings, top-N per group, period-over-period comparisons, cumulative sums or deduplication with window functions. ([MuhammadHusnainAli/vector-agentic-skills](https://github.com/MuhammadHusnainAli/vector-agentic-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/programming/sql/sql-window-functions))

- **sqlite-naive-datetime-system-tz-silent-shift**: SQLite + SQLAlchemy `DateTime(timezone=True)` round-trip silently strips tzinfo. Downstream `.astimezone()` on the naive datetime then uses the SYSTEM tz, not UTC — silently shifts time-of-day classification by the local UTC offset (e.g. ~13h on a KST host, ~9h on JST, ~5h on EST). Symptom: bucket/window/cutoff classifiers (session hours, after-hours/overnight splits, daily aggregates) misclassify in production. Tests pass on the dev machine that happens to match the writer's intended tz; bug surfaces on any other host.
